# Third-party notices

Flashex 0.1.0 for macOS (Apple Silicon) contains the open-source software listed here. Each component is distributed under its own licence, reproduced below. Flashex itself is closed source; its terms are in [LICENSE](LICENSE).

This file is generated by `scripts/third-party-notices.sh` from `cargo tree` and `cargo metadata` (the crates compiled into `flashex-app`, `flashex` and `flashex-keeper` for `aarch64-apple-darwin`) and from the font and icon licences bundled with the app. Do not edit it by hand.

## Fonts and icons

| Component | Licence | Source |
|---|---|---|
| Geist and Geist Mono | SIL Open Font License 1.1 | https://github.com/vercel/geist-font |
| Symbols Nerd Font Mono | MIT (the font); the icons inside keep their own licences, listed in the text | https://github.com/ryanoasis/nerd-fonts |
| Lucide icons | ISC | https://lucide.dev |
